Here\\'s a step-by-step explanation and the corresponding Python implementation:\\n\\n1. **Construct MST**: Start by adding all edges to the MST to connect every key vertex to every other key vertex. This ensures that each key vertex is connected to two edges in the original MST.\\n\\n5. **Select Centers Again**: Repeat steps 3 and 4 to select the two key vertices with the smallest possible distances in the newly created MST.\\n\\nHere\\'s the complete Python implementation:\\n\\n```python\\nclass ConnectedComponents:\\n    def __init__(self, N):\\n        # This yields a labelled recursive tree (not\n        uniformly random among all N^(N-2) trees, but diverse enough for\n        training).\n        \"\"\"\n        edges: List[Tuple[int, int]] = []\n        for i in range(1, N):\n            parent = rng.randint(0, i - 1)\n            edges.append((i, parent))\n        return edges\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _build_adj(N: int, edges: List[Tuple[int, int]]) -> List[List[int]]:\n        adj: List[List[int]] = [[] for _ in range(N)]\n        for u, v in edges:\n            adj[u].append(v)\n            adj[v].append(u)\n        return adj\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _bfs_distances(\n        N: int, adj: List[List[int]], sources: List[int]\n    ) -> List[int]:\n        \"\"\"Multi-source BFS returning shortest distance to any source.\"\"\"\n        INF = 10 ** 9\n        dist = [INF] * N\n        from collections import deque\n\n        q = deque()\n        for s in sources:\n            dist[s] = 0\n            q.append(s)\n        while q:\n            u = q.popleft()\n            for v in adj[u]:\n                if dist[v] > dist[u] + 1:\n                    dist[v] = dist[u] + 1\n                    q.append(v)\n        return dist\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _can_cover(\n        N: int,\n        adj: List[List[int]],\n        key_set: Set[int],\n        D: int,\n        M: int,\n    ) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Return True if M centers suffice to cover all key vertices within distance D.\n\n        Classic greedy DP on tree (BOI 2003 \"Dynamite\" style).\n        \"\"\"\n        if D < 0:\n            return False\n        if M >= len(key_set):\n            return True  #
